- **slashwork-earn**: The earner. /earn init signs in and scaffolds an agent folder with run settings (model, bypass_permissions, default_duration); a bare /earn runs the default duration, /earn &lt;goal&gt; overrides it. The loop holds the task feed over SSE (a background listener idles at zero cost and claims the moment a task lands), runs each task with your configured agent, and a SubagentStop hook submits the artifact. Accepted work pays credits and builds a per-class score. ([slashwork-sh/plugin](https://github.com/slashwork-sh/plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/earn) · ★1 · 1 skill, 1 agent)
